# Router Benchmark

## Environment

- Date: 2026-05-29
- Git commit: 9a341c8049cb7e1cc3fc50d485d17eb31239dc6f
- Node: v24.16.0
- NODE_ENV: production
- pnpm: 10.19.0
- Platform: linux x64
- CPU: AMD EPYC 7763 64-Core Processor (4)
- Memory: 16766418944 bytes
- Package versions:
  - @builder.io/qwik: 1.19.2
  - @qwik.dev/core: 2.0.0-beta.35
  - @qwik.dev/router: 2.0.0-beta.35
  - marko: 5.38.39
  - next: 16.2.6
  - react: 19.2.6
  - react-dom: 19.2.6
  - solid-js: 1.9.12
  - solid-js-2: 2.0.0-beta.13

## Rankings

### app render 1000 nodes

Renders a production app route that emits 1,000 simple text spans.

| rank | framework | case | value | diff vs 1st | unit |
| ---: | --- | --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| 1 | **mreact-app-router+log enabled** | app render 1000 nodes | 1348 | best | ops/sec |
| 2 | **mreact-app-router** | app render 1000 nodes | 1321 | -2% | ops/sec |
| 3 | **mreact-app-router+mreact react-compat** | app render 1000 nodes | 854 | -36.65% | ops/sec |
| 4 | marko-run | app render 1000 nodes | 845 | -37.31% | ops/sec |
| 5 | tanstack-start | app render 1000 nodes | 782 | -41.99% | ops/sec |
| 6 | qwik-city | app render 1000 nodes | 477 | -64.61% | ops/sec |
| 7 | solid-start | app render 1000 nodes | 401 | -70.25% | ops/sec |
| 8 | qwik-router-v2 | app render 1000 nodes | 380 | -71.81% | ops/sec |
| 9 | tanstack-start-solid | app render 1000 nodes | 337 | -75% | ops/sec |
| 10 | next-app-router | app render 1000 nodes | 80 | -94.07% | ops/sec |
